\doxysubsection*{\+: clock name}

struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structclk__init__data}{clk\+\_\+init\+\_\+data}} -\/ holds init data that\textquotesingle{}s common to all clocks and is shared between the clock provider and the common clock framework.
